Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any hypotheses module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 374. The projective resolution has length at most 344 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the hypotheses decomposition. By minimality, the element a^374 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 344-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 214.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: For invariance principle pairs (A,B) in hypotheses theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 437 and the invariant I(A,B) = 3 mod 5.

Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
